    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.parentElement;
    while (parent) {
      var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
      if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
      parent = parent.parentElement;
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

data-custom-css-path="custom.css">/*
 * ComfyUI docs — custom theme overrides (custom.css)
 *
 * Color tokens
 * ────────────
 * --ink-900:         #151417  dark-mode nav shell (replaces Mintlify charcoal #211927)
 * --hub-shell-light: #f7f6f9  light-mode nav shell (replaces sidebar #ffffff vs body mismatch)
 * --accent-purple:   #49378b  Comfy brand purple — active nav text, headings, CTA outline
 * --cta-text-dark:   #1a1a1a  dark-mode label on yellow primary button
 *
 * Light-mode sidebar default text stays Mintlify gray #404042 (rgb 64,64,66).
 * Only the current page link gets #49378b + Mintlify lavender pill rgba(73,55,139,0.1).
 */

:root {
  --ink-900: #151417;
  --hub-shell-light: #f7f6f9;
  --accent-purple: #49378b;
}

/* ── Dark mode ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Primary CTA: keep dark text (#1a1a1a) on yellow button */
html.dark .bg-primary-dark span {
  color: #1a1a1a !important;
}

/* Nav shell: topbar + sidebar → #151417 (was #211927) */
html.dark #navbar,
html.dark #sidebar,
html.dark #sidebar-content,
html.dark navbar-link,
html.dark navbar {
  background-color: var(--ink-900) !important;
}

html.dark .almond-layout,
html.dark .almond-layout > div,
html.dark body {
  background-color: var(--ink-900) !important;
}

html.dark #almond-background-color {
  background-color: var(--ink-900) !important;
}

/* ── Light mode — nav shell ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Unify topbar + sidebar with page shell → #f7f6f9 (was #ffffff on sidebar) */
html:not(.dark) #navbar,
html:not(.dark) #sidebar,
html:not(.dark) #sidebar-content,
html:not(.dark) navbar-link,
html:not(.dark) navbar {
  background-color: var(--hub-shell-light) !important;
}

html:not(.dark) .almond-layout,
html:not(.dark) .almond-layout > div,
html:not(.dark) body {
  background-color: var(--hub-shell-light) !important;
}

html:not(.dark) #almond-background-color {
  background-color: var(--hub-shell-light) !important;
}

/* ── Light mode — active nav (top tabs + dropdown) ─────────────────────────── */

/*
 * Active tab / dropdown item: #49378b text, no full-width fill.
 * Does NOT target sidebar groups — expanded sections keep default #404042 text.
 */
html:not(.dark) .nav-tabs-item[data-active],
html:not(.dark) mobile-nav-tabs-item[data-active],
html:not(.dark) nav-dropdown-item[data-active] {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
  color: #49378b !important;
  font-weight: 600 !important;
}

html:not(.dark) .nav-tabs-item[data-active] *,
html:not(.dark) mobile-nav-tabs-item[data-active] *,
html:not(.dark) nav-dropdown-item[data-active] * {
  color: inherit !important;
}

/* ── Light mode — sidebar current page only ─────────────────────────────────── */

/*
 * Mintlify marks expanded groups with [data-active], which previously forced
 * #49378b on every child. Target only the current page link (aria-current="page"):
 *   • active link  → #49378b + Mintlify pill rgba(73,55,139,0.1)
 *   • siblings     → default #404042 (theme, unchanged)
 */
html:not(.dark) #sidebar-content li:has(> a[aria-current="page"]) {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
}

html:not(.dark) #sidebar-content a[aria-current="page"] {
  color: #49378b !important;
  font-weight: 600 !important;
}

html:not(.dark) #sidebar-content a[aria-current="page"] * {
  color: inherit !important;
}

/* ── Light mode — right TOC ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Current heading in page TOC: #49378b, no block fill */
html:not(.dark) toc-item[data-active],
html:not(.dark) toc-item[data-active-deepest],
html:not(.dark) toc-item[data-active] a,
html:not(.dark) toc-item[data-active-deepest] a {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  color: #49378b !important;
  font-weight: 600 !important;
}

/* ── Light mode — content headings ─────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* All article headings h1–h6 → #49378b */
html:not(.dark) #header :where(h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6),
html:not(.dark) #content-area :where(h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6) {
  color: #49378b !important;
}

/* ── Light mode — Comfy Cloud CTA ───────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Navbar Cloud button: #49378b text + #49378b border, transparent fill (no neon yellow) */
html:not(.dark) #navbar a[href*="comfy.org/cloud"] {
  background-color: transparent !important;
  color: #49378b !important;
  border: 1px solid #49378b !important;
  box-shadow: none !important;
}

html:not(.dark) #navbar a[href*="comfy.org/cloud"] * {
  color: inherit !important;
}

@container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="MiniMax H3: ComfyUI Workflow Examples" data-page-href="/tutorials/video/minimax/minimax-h3" id="content"><span data-as="p"><a href="https://www.minimax.io/blog/minimax-h3" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">MiniMax H3</a> is MiniMax’s general-purpose, omni-modal generation model, now available as open weights. It jointly understands text, images, video, and audio in a single context, and generates video with <strong>native stereo audio</strong>: voice, sound effects, and music are modeled together in a single forward pass instead of being layered on afterward. Output is up to 2K resolution, 24fps, and about 15 seconds.</span>
<span data-as="p">ComfyUI natively supports MiniMax H3. The template library currently ships with three example workflows, each covering one generation mode:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Text-to-Video</strong> (T2V): Generate videos from text prompts</li>
<li><strong>Image-to-Video</strong> (I2V): Generate videos from an input image, with optional first/last-frame control</li>
<li><strong>Reference-to-Video</strong> (R2V): Generate videos that lock in a character, style, motion, camera move, or voice from reference images, videos, and audio</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">These three are example templates, not an exhaustive list. The model supports more generation modes through the native MiniMax H3 nodes: first/last-frame image-to-video (fl2va) via the <code>MiniMaxH3ImageToVideo</code> node, and reference-driven generation with images, videos, and audio (ref2va) via the <code>MiniMaxH3ReferenceToVideo</code> node. You can build additional workflows with these nodes.</span>
<!-- -->
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="key-features"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#key-features" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Key features</span></h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Native stereo audio</strong>: Dialogue, sound effects, and music are generated together with the video, synced in one MP4</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal context</strong>: Text, images, video, and audio references can be combined in one generation</li>
<li><strong>Reference-driven generation</strong>: Lock a character’s identity, a style, a motion, a camera move, or a voice from reference materials</li>
<li><strong>Instruction following</strong>: Describe the relationship between references and the target shot in natural language</li>
<li><strong>Accurate text rendering</strong>: Spelled-out text and brand elements render cleanly</li>
<li><strong>Open weights</strong>: Run locally in ComfyUI with full control over every parameter</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="getting-started"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#getting-started" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Getting started</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">MiniMax H3 is supported in ComfyUI with open weights. To get started:</span>
<ol>
<li>Update ComfyUI to version 0.30.0 or later</li>
<li>Go to <strong>Template Library</strong> &gt; <strong>Video</strong> &gt; choose any MiniMax H3 workflow</li>
<li>Follow the pop-up to download models and run the workflow</li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">The model files are hosted on Hugging Face in the <a href="https://huggingface.co/Comfy-Org/MiniMax-H3" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Comfy-Org/MiniMax-H3</a> repository.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="setting-the-output-resolution"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#setting-the-output-resolution" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Setting the output resolution</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Each workflow uses a <strong>Resolution Selector</strong> node to control the overall output size. The node computes <code>width</code> and <code>height</code> from three settings, and its outputs connect directly to the <code>width</code> and <code>height</code> inputs of the MiniMax H3 node:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Aspect ratio</strong>: Pick a preset such as <code>16:9 (Widescreen)</code>, <code>9:16 (Portrait Widescreen)</code>, or <code>1:1 (Square)</code></li>
<li><strong>Megapixels</strong>: Target total pixel count for the output. Higher values give larger frames; lower values run faster</li>
<li><strong>Multiple</strong>: The computed resolution is rounded to the nearest multiple of this number. Keep it at <code>32</code> to match H3’s resolution grid</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The template ships with a fast preview size. For full-quality output, raise Megapixels to about <code>1.0</code> at 16:9, which yields roughly 1344x768: H3’s native canvas, a 768px short edge capped at 768x1344 pixels.</span>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prompting-tips"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prompting-tips" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Prompting tips</span></h4>
<ol>
<li><strong>Describe the whole scene</strong>: State the overall scene first (location, character, what is happening), then break it into timed shots</li>
<li><strong>Shots, camera, and audio</strong>: Describe the shots, camera moves, and the accompanying audio (dialogue, SFX, music) in one prompt block</li>
<li><strong>Resolution</strong>: H3’s native canvas is a 768px short edge, capped at 768x1344 pixels and rounded to a multiple of 32</li>
<li><strong>Duration</strong>: The duration input snaps to the model’s 17-frame-per-block (17k+5) grid at 24fps</li>
<li><strong>First/last frame</strong>: Connect images to <code>first_frame</code> and/or <code>last_frame</code> on the <code>MiniMaxH3ImageToVideo</code> node to turn this workflow into first/last-frame image-to-video</li>
</ol>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prompt-writing-guide"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prompt-writing-guide" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Prompt writing guide</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">MiniMax publishes an official <a href="https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-H3/blob/main/docs/VIDEO_PROMPT_WRITING_GUIDE_base_en.md" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">video prompt writing guide</a> for the base generation modes: T2VA, I2VA, FL2VA, and L2VA. The guide explains how to structure a prompt into timed shots with camera movement and audio (dialogue, SFX, music), and includes examples for each mode.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="minimax-h3-image-to-video-i2v"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#minimax-h3-image-to-video-i2v" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">MiniMax H3 Image to Video (I2V)</span></h3>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prompting-tips-2"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prompting-tips-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Prompting tips</span></h4>
<ol>
<li><strong>Keyframes</strong>: The <code>first_frame</code> and <code>last_frame</code> inputs are optional; the model generates the motion between them</li>
<li><strong>Prompt</strong>: Describe the shots, motion, and the accompanying audio (dialogue, SFX, music) in one block</li>
<li><strong>Resolution</strong>: H3’s native canvas is a 768px short edge, capped at 768x1344 pixels and rounded to a multiple of 32</li>
<li><strong>Duration</strong>: The duration input snaps to the model’s 17-frame-per-block (17k+5) grid at 24fps</li>
</ol>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prompt-writing-guide-2"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prompt-writing-guide-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Prompt writing guide</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">MiniMax publishes an official <a href="https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-H3/blob/main/docs/VIDEO_PROMPT_WRITING_GUIDE_base_en.md" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">video prompt writing guide</a> for the base generation modes: T2VA, I2VA, FL2VA, and L2VA. The guide explains how to structure a prompt into timed shots with camera movement and audio (dialogue, SFX, music), and includes examples for each mode.</span>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prompting-tips-3"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prompting-tips-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Prompting tips</span></h4>
<ol>
<li><strong>Reference by tag</strong>: Reference each input by tag in the exact order it was connected, for example <code>&lt;Picture 1&gt;</code>, <code>&lt;Video 1&gt;</code>, <code>&lt;Audio 1&gt;</code></li>
<li><strong>Assign each reference a job</strong>: State which reference drives which part of the shot (identity, style, motion, camera, voice). Explicit assignments tend to work much better</li>
<li><strong>Limits</strong>: Up to 9 reference images, 3 reference videos (each can carry its own soundtrack), and 3 standalone reference audio clips</li>
<li><strong>ref_image_size</strong>: <code>match</code> scales references down to the generation resolution for speed; <code>max</code> keeps up to a 2048px short edge for stronger identity fidelity at the cost of speed</li>
<li><strong>Note</strong>: R2V uses the <code>ref2va</code> diffusion model, a different set of weights from the <code>fl2va</code> model used by the T2V and I2V workflows</li>
</ol>
<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="prompt-writing-guide-3"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#prompt-writing-guide-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Prompt writing guide</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">MiniMax publishes an official <a href="https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-H3/blob/main/docs/VIDEO_PROMPT_WRITING_GUIDE_ref_en.md" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">full-reference mode prompt guide</a> for reference-driven generation (R2V). The guide covers the rewrite output structure, including subject definitions, reference labels, and retention analysis, and how to assign each reference a role in the target shot.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="speeding-up-generation-with-sage-attention"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#speeding-up-generation-with-sage-attention" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Speeding up generation with Sage Attention</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The example workflows use the standard attention implementation. You can roughly double the generation speed with <a href="https://github.com/woct0rdho/SageAttention" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Sage Attention</a>, with minimal quality loss. Sage Attention is an optional dependency, so you need to install it yourself:</span>
<ol>
<li>Install the <code>sageattention</code> Python package. Download the wheel that matches your PyTorch and CUDA versions from the <a href="https://github.com/woct0rdho/SageAttention/releases" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">SageAttention releases</a> page, then install it with <code>pip install &lt;wheel-file&gt;</code>.</li>
<li>Install the <a href="https://github.com/kijai/ComfyUI-KJNodes" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">KJNodes custom nodes</a>, which provide the <code>Patch Sage Attention KJ</code> node. Use the ComfyUI Manager, or clone the repository into <code>ComfyUI/custom_nodes/</code> and restart ComfyUI.</li>
<li>Add a <code>Patch Sage Attention KJ</code> node to the workflow and connect it between the <code>UNETLoader</code> and the <code>BasicGuider</code> node: its <code>model</code> input receives the model from the <code>UNETLoader</code>, and its <code>model</code> output feeds the <code>model</code> input of the <code>BasicGuider</code>. Set <code>sage_attention</code> to <code>auto</code>.</li>
<li>Run the workflow as usual. Only the guider needs the patch; the scheduler only generates the sigmas and can stay as is.</li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">Notes:</span>
<ul>
<li>Sage Attention requires float16 or bfloat16 tensors. MiniMax H3 runs some layers in other dtypes, so you may see “Input tensors must be in dtype of torch.float16 or torch.bfloat16, using pytorch attention instead” messages in the console. These are expected; the affected layers fall back to standard attention and generation still works.</li>
<li>Alternatively, you can enable Sage Attention globally by launching ComfyUI with the <code>--use-sage-attention</code> flag instead of adding the node.</li>
</ul></div><div class="feedback-toolbar pb-16 w-full flex flex-col gap-y-8"><div class="flex flex-row flex-wrap gap-4 items-center justify-between"><p class="inline-block text-sm text-gray-600 document.head.appendChild(style);\n  }\n  \n  // Remove existing giscus instances\n  function cleanupGiscus() {\n    const existingContainers = document.querySelectorAll('.giscus-container');\n    existingContainers.forEach(container =\u003e container.remove());\n    \n    // Also remove any orphaned giscus elements\n    const giscusElements = document.querySelectorAll('.giscus, giscus-widget, (intersectionObserver) {\n      intersectionObserver.disconnect();\n      intersectionObserver = null;\n    }\n    \n    if (themeObserver) {\n      themeObserver.disconnect();\n      themeObserver = null;\n    }\n    \n    if (loadingTimeout) {\n      clearTimeout(loadingTimeout);\n      loadingTimeout = null;\n    }\n\n    if (routeLoadTimer) {\n      clearTimeout(routeLoadTimer);\n      routeLoadTimer = null;\n    }\n\n    if (intersectionLoadTimer) {\n      clearTimeout(intersectionLoadTimer);\n      intersectionLoadTimer = null;\n    }\n    \n    giscusLoaded = false;\n  }\n\n  // Create placeholder for lazy loading\n  function createGiscusPlaceholder() {\n    const isChinesePage = window.location.pathname.includes('/zh/') || window.location.pathname.includes('/cn/');\n    const placeholderText = isChinesePage \n      ? '💬 点击或滚动到此处加载评论'\n      : '💬 Click or scroll here to load comments';\n    \n    const placeholder = document.createElement('div');\n    placeholder.className = 'giscus-placeholder';\n    placeholder.innerHTML = `\n      \u003cdiv class=\"giscus-placeholder-content\"\u003e\n        \u003cdiv class=\"giscus-placeholder-icon\"\u003e💬\u003c/div\u003e\n        \u003cdiv\u003e${placeholderText}\u003c/div\u003e\n      \u003c/div\u003e\n    `;\n    \n    // Click to load\n    placeholder.addEventListener('click', () =\u003e {\n      loadGiscusContent(placeholder.parentElement);\n    });\n    \n    return placeholder;\n  }\n\n  // Setup intersection observer for lazy loading\n  function setupLazyLoading(container) {\n    if (!window.IntersectionObserver || giscusLoaded) return;\n    \n    intersectionObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) =\u003e {\n      entries.forEach(entry =\u003e {\n        if (entry.isIntersecting \u0026\u0026 !giscusLoaded) {\n          if (!intersectionLoadTimer) {\n            intersectionLoadTimer = setTimeout(() =\u003e {\n              intersectionLoadTimer = null;\n              if (giscusLoaded) return;\n              loadGiscusContent(container);\n              if (intersectionObserver) {\n                intersectionObserver.disconnect();\n                intersectionObserver = null;\n              }\n            }, INTERSECTION_LOAD_DELAY_MS);\n          }\n        } else if (intersectionLoadTimer) {\n          clearTimeout(intersectionLoadTimer);\n          intersectionLoadTimer = null;\n        }\n      });\n    }, {\n      rootMargin: INTERSECTION_ROOT_MARGIN,\n      threshold: 0.15\n    });\n    \n    intersectionObserver.observe(container);\n  }\n\n  function waitForWindowLoad() {\n    if (document.readyState === 'complete') {\n      return Promise.resolve();\n    }\n\n    return new Promise((resolve) =\u003e {\n      window.addEventListener('load', resolve, { once: true });\n    });\n  }\n\n  function waitForNextPaint() {\n    return new Promise((resolve) =\u003e {\n      requestAnimationFrame(() =\u003e {\n        requestAnimationFrame(resolve);\n      });\n    });\n  }\n\n  async function waitForPageContentStability() {\n    await waitForWindowLoad();\n    await waitForNextPaint();\n\n    const contentRoot = document.querySelector('.mdx-content.prose, .mdx-content, main, [role=\"main\"]');\n    if (!contentRoot) return;\n\n    await new Promise((resolve) =\u003e {\n      const startedAt = Date.now();\n      let stableForMs = 0;\n      let previousSignature = '';\n\n      const checkStability = () =\u003e {\n        const signature = [\n          contentRoot.childElementCount,\n          contentRoot.scrollHeight,\n          contentRoot.clientHeight,\n          (contentRoot.textContent || '').trim().length\n        ].join(':');\n\n        if (signature === previousSignature) {\n          stableForMs += CONTENT_STABILITY_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS;\n        } else {\n          stableForMs = 0;\n          previousSignature = signature;\n        }\n\n        const waitedTooLong = Date.now() - startedAt \u003e= CONTENT_STABILITY_MAX_WAIT_MS;\n        if (stableForMs \u003e= CONTENT_STABILITY_REQUIRED_MS || waitedTooLong) {\n          resolve();\n          return;\n        }\n\n        setTimeout(checkStability, CONTENT_STABILITY_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS);\n      };\n\n      checkStability();\n    });\n  }\n\n  function scheduleGiscusLoad(delayMs) {\n    const scheduledPath = window.location.pathname;\n\n    if (routeLoadTimer) {\n      clearTimeout(routeLoadTimer);\n    }\n\n    routeLoadTimer = setTimeout(async () =\u003e {\n      routeLoadTimer = null;\n\n      if (window.location.pathname !== scheduledPath) return;\n\n      await waitForPageContentStability();\n\n      if (window.location.pathname !== scheduledPath) return;\n\n      loadGiscus();\n    }, delayMs);\n  }\n  \n  // Find the best content container for giscus\n  function findContentContainer() {\n    // Wait for content to be ready\n    return new Promise((resolve) =\u003e {\n      let attempts = 0;\n      const maxAttempts = 20;\n      \n      function tryFind() {\n        // Specific selectors for Mintlify content structure\n        const selectors = [\n          // Mintlify main content container - this is the key one!\n          '.mdx-content.prose',\n          '.mdx-content',\n          // Fallback selectors\n          'main [class*=\"prose\"]:not([class*=\"nav\"]):not([class*=\"sidebar\"])',\n          'main article:not([class*=\"nav\"])',\n          'main \u003e div \u003e div:last-child', // Common Mintlify structure\n          '[role=\"main\"] \u003e div:last-child',\n          'main .content',\n          'main'\n        ];\n        \n        for (const selector of selectors) {\n          const elements = document.querySelectorAll(selector);\n          for (const element of elements) {\n            const textContent = element.textContent?.trim() || '';\n            const noExistingGiscus = !element.querySelector('.giscus-container');\n            \n            // Special handling for .mdx-content - this is our primary target\n            if (element.classList.contains('mdx-content')) {\n              if (textContent.length \u003e 50 \u0026\u0026 noExistingGiscus) {\n                console.log('Giscus: Found mdx-content container', element);\n                resolve(element);\n                return;\n              }\n            } else {\n              // Stricter requirements for other selectors\n              const hasSubstantialContent = textContent.length \u003e 200;\n              const notNavigation = !element.closest('[class*=\"nav\"], nav, .sidebar');\n              \n              if (hasSubstantialContent \u0026\u0026 notNavigation \u0026\u0026 noExistingGiscus) {\n                console.log('Giscus: Found content container', selector, element);\n                resolve(element);\n                return;\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n        \n        attempts++;\n        if (attempts \u003c maxAttempts) {\n          setTimeout(tryFind, 250);\n        } else {\n          resolve(null);\n        }\n      }\n      \n      tryFind();\n    });\n  }\n  \n  // Get current theme from Mintlify\n  function getCurrentTheme() {\n    // Check for Mintlify's theme class on html or body\n    const html = document.documentElement;\n    const body = document.body;\n    \n    // Mintlify typically uses data attributes or classes\n    if (html.classList.contains('dark') || body.classList.contains('dark')) {\n      return 'dark';\n    } else if (html.classList.contains('light') || body.classList.contains('light')) {\n      return 'light';\n    } else if (html.dataset.theme === 'dark' || body.dataset.theme === 'dark') {\n      return 'dark';\n    } else if (html.dataset.theme === 'light' || body.dataset.theme === 'light') {\n      return 'light';\n    }\n    \n    // Fallback to system preference\n    return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light';\n  }\n\n  // Update giscus theme\n  function updateGiscusTheme(theme) {\n    const giscusFrame = document.querySelector('iframe.giscus-frame');\n    if (giscusFrame) {\n      const message = {\n        giscus: {\n          setConfig: {\n            theme: theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n          }\n        }\n      };\n      giscusFrame.contentWindow.postMessage(message, 'https://giscus.app');\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Set up theme change observer\n  function setupThemeObserver() {\n    // Don't create multiple observers\n    if (themeObserver) {\n      return;\n    }\n    \n    // Watch for changes to the document element's attributes and classes\n    themeObserver = new MutationObserver((mutations) =\u003e {\n      let themeChanged = false;\n      \n      mutations.forEach((mutation) =\u003e {\n        if (mutation.type === 'attributes' \u0026\u0026 \n            (mutation.attributeName === 'class' || \n             mutation.attributeName === 'data-theme' ||\n             mutation.attributeName === 'data-color-scheme')) {\n          themeChanged = true;\n        }\n      });\n      \n      if (themeChanged) {\n        const newTheme = getCurrentTheme();\n        updateGiscusTheme(newTheme);\n      }\n    });\n\n    // Observe both html and body elements for theme changes\n    themeObserver.observe(document.documentElement, {\n      attributes: true,\n      attributeFilter: ['class', 'data-theme', 'data-color-scheme']\n    });\n    \n    themeObserver.observe(document.body, {\n      attributes: true,\n      attributeFilter: ['class', 'data-theme', 'data-color-scheme']\n    });\n\n    // Also listen for system theme changes\n    const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)');\n    mediaQuery.addEventListener('change', () =\u003e {\n      // Only update if no explicit theme is set\n      const html = document.documentElement;\n      const body = document.body;\n      if (!html.classList.contains('dark') \u0026\u0026 !html.classList.contains('light') \u0026\u0026\n          !body.classList.contains('dark') \u0026\u0026 !body.classList.contains('light') \u0026\u0026\n          !html.dataset.theme \u0026\u0026 !body.dataset.theme) {\n        const newTheme = getCurrentTheme();\n        updateGiscusTheme(newTheme);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Generate GitHub Discussions URL for current page\n  function generateDiscussionUrl() {\n    const repoOwner = 'Comfy-Org';\n    const repoName = 'docs';\n    const currentPath = window.location.pathname;\n    const currentTitle = document.title || 'Discussion';\n    const currentUrl = window.location.href;\n    \n    // Extract meaningful part of the path for search\n    const pathSegments = currentPath.split('/').filter(Boolean);\n    let searchQuery = '';\n    \n    if (pathSegments.length \u003e 0) {\n      // Use the complete path as search term, keeping language prefixes\n      // Example: /zh/development/core-concepts/workflow -\u003e \"zh development core concepts workflow\"\n      searchQuery = pathSegments\n        .join(' ')\n        .replace(/[-_]/g, ' ') // Replace dashes/underscores with spaces\n        .replace(/\\s+/g, ' ') // Normalize multiple spaces\n        .trim();\n    }\n    \n    // If no meaningful path, use page title\n    if (!searchQuery \u0026\u0026 currentTitle) {\n      searchQuery = currentTitle.replace(/[^\\w\\s]/g, '').trim();\n    }\n    \n    // Create search URL to find existing discussions\n    if (searchQuery) {\n      const encodedQuery = encodeURIComponent(searchQuery);\n      return `https://github.com/${repoOwner}/${repoName}/discussions?discussions_q=${encodedQuery}`;\n    }\n    \n    // Fallback to discussions main page\n    return `https://github.com/${repoOwner}/${repoName}/discussions`;\n  }\n  \n  // Show friendly discussion notice\n  function showGiscusNotice(container, noticeType = 'rate_limit') {\n    const discussionUrl function() {\n    const container = document.querySelector('.giscus-container');\n    if (!container) return;\n    \n    // Simply reload the giscus content\n    giscusLoaded = false; // Reset the loaded flag\n    loadGiscusContent(container);\n  };\n\n  // Listen for giscus messages to detect rate limit errors\n  function setupGiscusErrorListener() {\n    window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {\n      if (event.origin !== 'https://giscus.app') return;\n      \n      const message = event.data;\n      console.log('Giscus message received:', message); // Debug log\n      \n      const container = document.querySelector('.giscus-container');\n      if (!container) return;\n      \n      // Check for various error patterns\n      if (message \u0026\u0026 message.giscus) {\n        // Standard giscus error format\n        if (message.giscus.error) {\n          const errorType = message.giscus.error.type;\n          console.warn('Giscus error detected:', errorType, message.giscus.error);\n          \n          if (errorType === 'rate_limit' || errorType === 'rate-limit' || errorType === 'RATE_LIMITED') {\n            showGiscusNotice(container, 'rate_limit');\n          } else {\n            showGiscusNotice(container, 'network');\n          }\n        }\n        \n        // Check for discussion data with error indicators\n        if (message.giscus.discussion === null \u0026\u0026 message.giscus.viewer === null) {\n          // This might indicate a rate limit or auth issue\n          console.warn('Giscus: No discussion data, possibly rate limited');\n          setTimeout(() =\u003e {\n            if (container.querySelector('.giscus-frame')) {\n              const iframe = container.querySelector('.giscus-frame');\n              if (iframe \u0026\u0026 iframe.contentDocument) {\n                const errorElements = iframe.contentDocument.querySelectorAll('[class*=\"error\"], [class*=\"Error\"]');\n                if (errorElements.length \u003e 0) {\n                  console.warn('Giscus: Error elements found in iframe');\n                  showGiscusNotice(container, 'rate_limit');\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          }, 3000);\n        }\n      }\n      \n      // Also check for direct error messages in the content\n      if (typeof message === 'string' \u0026\u0026 message.includes('rate limit')) {\n        console.warn('Giscus: Rate limit detected in string message');\n        showGiscusNotice(container, 'rate_limit');\n      }\n    });\n    \n    // Additional error detection: Monitor iframe content changes\n    const checkForErrors = () =\u003e {\n      const container = document.querySelector('.giscus-container');\n      const iframe = document.querySelector('.giscus-frame');\n      \n      if (iframe \u0026\u0026 container) {\n        try {\n          // Check if iframe has loaded and has error content\n          setTimeout(() =\u003e {\n            const iframeDoc = iframe.contentDocument || iframe.contentWindow.document;\n            if (iframeDoc) {\n              const bodyText = iframeDoc.body ? iframeDoc.body.textContent || '' : '';\n              if (bodyText.includes('rate limit') || bodyText.includes('API rate limit') || bodyText.includes('exceeded')) {\n                console.warn('Giscus: Rate limit detected in iframe content');\n                showGiscusNotice(container, 'rate_limit');\n              } else if (bodyText.includes('error occurred')) {\n                console.warn('Giscus: Generic error detected in iframe content');\n                showGiscusNotice(container, 'rate_limit'); // Assume it's rate limit for now\n              }\n            }\n          }, 5000); // Check after 5 seconds\n        } catch (e) {\n          // Cross-origin restrictions, can't access iframe content\n          console.log('Cannot access iframe content due to CORS');\n        }\n      }\n    };\n    \n    // Run error check periodically\n    setTimeout(checkForErrors, 6000);\n  }\n\n  // Load actual giscus content\n  function loadGiscusContent(container) {\n    if (giscusLoaded) return;\n    giscusLoaded = true;\n    \n    // Clear placeholder\n    container.innerHTML = '';\n    container.className = 'giscus-container loading';\n    \n    // Get // Replace with real ID\n    script.setAttribute('data-category', 'Announcements');\n    script.setAttribute('data-category-id', 'DIC_kwDOLlassc4CtQoz'); // Replace with real ID\n    script.setAttribute('data-mapping', 'pathname');\n    script.setAttribute('data-strict', '1');\n    script.setAttribute('data-reactions-enabled', '1');\n    script.setAttribute('data-emit-metadata', '0');\n    script.setAttribute('data-input-position', 'bottom');\n    script.setAttribute('data-theme', currentTheme === 'dark' ? loadingTimeout = null;\n      }\n      \n      setTimeout(() =\u003e {\n        container.classList.remove('loading');\n        container.classList.add('loaded');\n        \n        // Set up theme showGiscusNotice(container, 'network');\n    };\n    \n    // Set up a timeout to detect if giscus fails to load\n    loadingTimeout = setTimeout(() =\u003e {\n      // Check if giscus is still (container.classList.contains('loading') || (iframe \u0026\u0026 !hasError)) {\n        // Check iframe content for errors if possible\n        try {\n          if (iframe \u0026\u0026 iframe.contentDocument) {\n            const iframeDoc = iframe.contentDocument;\n            const bodyText = iframeDoc.body ? iframeDoc.body.textContent || '' : '';\n            \n            if You can roughly double the generation speed with `,_jsx(_components.a,{href:`https://github.com/woct0rdho/SageAttention`,children:`Sage Attention`}),`, with minimal quality loss. Sage Attention is an optional dependency, so you need to install it yourself:`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ol,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`Install the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`sageattention`}),` Python package. Use the ComfyUI Manager, or clone the repository into `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ComfyUI/custom_nodes/`}),` and restart ComfyUI.`]}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`Add a `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`Patch Sage Attention KJ`}),` node to the workflow and connect it between the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`UNETLoader`}),` and the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BasicGuider`}),` node: its `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`model`}),` input receives the model from the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`UNETLoader`}),`, and its `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`model`}),` output feeds the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`model`}),` input of the `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`BasicGuider`}),`. Set `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`sage_attention`}),` to `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`auto`}),`.`]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.li,{children:`Run the workflow as usual. Only the guider needs the patch; the scheduler only generates the sigmas and can stay as is.`}),`\n`]}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`Notes:`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ul,{children:[`\n`,_jsx(_components.li,{children:`Sage Attention requires float16 or bfloat16 tensors. MiniMax H3 runs some layers in other dtypes, so you may see “Input tensors must be in dtype of torch.float16 or torch.bfloat16, using pytorch attention instead” messages in the console. These are expected; the affected layers fall back to standard attention and generation still works.`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`Alternatively, you can enable Sage Attention globally by
